The later ayat broadened `الرجعى` from destination alone to response and course-reversal.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B001","root_000544/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3fed8014b51f64847e2c","text":"1. **Return to source, owner, and completion.** `ر ج ع:B001` supplies return to a prior place, owner, or state; `ر ب ب:B001` supplies ownership and authority, while `ر ب ب:B002` supplies nurture and completion. The directional `إلى` immediately reverses 96:7’s self-sufficient closure, and the earlier generous-teaching relation remains in the window (96:3–5). The Rabb functions both as cultivating giver and destination. I infer that return completes the dependency relation rather than merely changing location. The line reads as restoration to the authority and developmental source the self had mentally displaced.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000544/B001","root_000544/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_590756ad1d003e75d780","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:8:4\",\"root_000544\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Dönüş, kaçınılmaz sonun yanında yanlış bir eylemden ve yönden geri dönme imkânını da içinde taşır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B001","root_000544/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_772dfcee7d5f9aad7288","text":"1. The return is a reversal of self-sufficiency into Lord-directed accountability. Evidence: `ر ب ب B001` lordship/authority, `ر ب ب B002` nurture/completion, `ر ج ع B001` return/sending back, `ر ج ع B003` turning back from an act. Structural cue: `إلى ربك` front-loads the destination before `الرجعى`, answering 96:7. Mechanism: the same Lord who creates and teaches is the endpoint to which the human's claimed autonomy is returned. Abductive move: the packet supplies destination and return; I infer juridical and formative return together. Reading change: the ayah reads as both eschatic return and correction of a misdirected course. 2. Return also activates reply and repeated sound, because the packet is full of call-response patterns. Evidence: `ر ج ع B005` returned answer/speech, `ر ج ع B007` repetition of sound, later `د ع و B001` calling and 96:17-18 mirrored calls. Structural cue: 96:8 sits before the triple `أرأيت` sequence, which repeatedly sends the hearer back to reassess. Mechanism: return is not only spatial; it is dialogic, a reply demanded by the earlier command to read. Abductive move: the packet gives repeated speech forms; I infer that `الرجعى` also governs the text's call-and-answer movement. Reading change: return becomes an acoustic/intellectual recoil that forces reevaluation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000544/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_82af753a2ec1ec097f3d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:8:4\",\"root_000544\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Dönüş, buyruğa ve sorulara davranışla geri verilen bir cevap anlamı da kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8848911cb51888118706","text":"96:17-18 sharpened the return as counter-call: “let him call” is answered by “We shall call.” The return to the Lord becomes visible as a summons stronger than the human council's summons.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000544/B005","root_000544/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_90f3f0faf5a7fbae5121","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:8:4\",\"root_000544\",[\"B005\",\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Tekrarlanan sorular ve karşılıklı çağrılar, dönüşe yankılanan söz ve geri gönderilen ses boyutu ekler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000544/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c03f6e180a9e700cf294","text":"2. **Return as an answer sent back.** `ر ج ع:B005` supplies a returned answer or reply. The surrounding discourse repeatedly commands (`اقرأ`), then asks `أرأيت` and tests alternatives of guidance, protective command, denial, and turning (96:9–13). The speech structure gives return the role of response: conduct becomes the answer sent back to the Rabb. The packet supplies questions and directives; I infer accountability as the directional arrow joining them to `الرجعى`. The noun can therefore denote responsive reckoning as well as homeward movement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000544/B003","root_001684/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e81cca512f03341ebc7d","text":"3. **A corrective turning set against turning away.** `ر ج ع:B003` supplies turning back from an act or prior course. Within the window, 96:13 ends with `تولى`, whose `و ل ي:B007` is turning away. These are opposed vectors: one can reverse a wrong course, whereas the denier reverses away from guidance. I infer that the nominal `الرجعى` keeps corrective reversal latent even while asserting unavoidable return. The line reads simultaneously as destination and as the possibility or demand of course-correction.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
